How the section works
Choose how clients contact you, publish it, then handle the result in InkLinka.
The section contains forms, booking links and website chat. They solve different tasks but are configured and managed in one place.
Booking + calendar
Availability settings
Contact form
AI website chat
1. Choose the tool
Use booking with a calendar, a contact form, AI-supported website chat or a personal booking link.
2. Configure the rules
Select artists, availability, fields and appearance. Personal links can also use service deposit rules.
3. Publish it
Paste the embed code into your website, add a modal button or copy a direct link.
4. Process the request
Booking submissions appear as requests; contact form submissions appear in shared Conversations.
Booking form + calendar
The client chooses an artist, date and time and sends a booking request.
This widget combines availability and the booking questionnaire. It creates a new request for the studio to review; it does not silently turn an enquiry into a confirmed appointment.
Choose which artists and services are shown.
Collect name, contacts, tattoo details, preferences and up to 10 reference images.
Keep the selected date, time, artist and client answers in one request.

Availability and publishing
You control which dates clients see and how the form opens on the website.
Set the visible period and available time slots for selected artists. Existing bookings and active requests are taken into account, while dates and times can also be blocked manually.
Open or close individual dates and time slots.
Adjust calendar and form colors, borders, spacing and button text.
Use an embedded widget, a modal website button or a direct link.
Standalone contact form
Add enquiry, consultation or waiting-list forms to your website.
Create a form for a specific purpose, choose its fields and embed it on any website page. Every submission opens a conversation in the shared Inbox.
Use standard fields or add custom required and optional fields.
Collect contact details, request type, tattoo idea, placement, budget, preferred artist and a message.
See the source page and campaign data when tracking is enabled.

Website chat with AI first replies
Answer common studio questions before a manager joins the conversation.
Add a chat widget to your website and enable the studio knowledge base with AI. It can answer basic questions about hours, consultations, styles and the booking process using approved studio information and conversation context.
Set the greeting, color, position and whether to collect the visitor’s name, email or phone.
Add studio answers to the knowledge base and use AI to formulate a relevant first reply.
When an answer needs a person, the conversation is marked for the team and remains in shared Conversations.
The website chat widget is available on Pro and Expert plans.

Personal booking links
Publish a complete booking page without adding anything to a website.
Create a direct URL for one artist, several artists or a specific booking campaign. The page contains the same calendar and booking form and can be shared in social profiles or messages.
Choose the URL, artists, working hours, available dates and form fields.
Configure slot duration and deposit rules for individual services.
Duplicate links when you need a separate version for another artist or campaign.

What you get
Forms, booking links and website chat — managed from one section.
Use the booking widget, contact form, AI-supported chat and personal links separately or together. Every request and conversation remains available to the team inside InkLinka. Configuration is done on a computer; client communication is available on a phone.
Set up forms and widgets
Questions and answers
Website widgets and booking links: common questions
Practical details about publishing, availability, submissions and team follow-up.
Can I add these tools to an existing website? Yes. Embed the booking widget or contact forms on a website page, add a button that opens the booking form in a modal, and install the AI chat as a website widget. Personal booking links are separate pages intended for social profiles and messages.
Does a booking form submission create a confirmed appointment? No. It creates a booking request with the selected artist, date, time and client answers. The studio reviews the request and decides whether to confirm it or contact the client first.
How does the booking calendar decide which times to show? You choose the artists, visible period and available time slots. The calendar takes existing events and active requests into account, and the studio can also block individual dates or times manually.
How is a contact form different from the booking form? A contact form has no calendar or deposit step. It uses standard or custom required and optional fields, does not collect reference images, and creates a new conversation in the shared Inbox.
What are personal booking links used for? They are standalone booking pages for one artist, several artists or a specific campaign. Share them in an Instagram profile or message; each page can use its own availability, fields, slot duration and service deposit rules.
What can the AI website chat answer? It can answer basic questions about studio hours, consultations, styles and the booking process using the approved studio knowledge base. When a question needs a person, a manager can join the same conversation. The widget is available on Pro and Expert plans.

FIRST HOURS\n\nIf the tattoo is covered with film (including second skin), do not remove it for the time recommended by the artist.\n\nIf the film is regular — remove it 2–4 hours after the session.\n\n2. CLOTHING\n\nWear clean, loose clothing made of natural fabrics.\n\nAvoid friction and pressure on the tattooed area.\n\n6. WHAT NOT TO DO\n\nDo not scratch or pick at scabs or peeling.\n\nDo not cover the tattoo with tight bandages unnecessarily.\n\nDo not apply makeup or sunscreen until fully healed.\n\n7.
